### [1] [From populations to absolute binding affinities in molecular simulations: exact volumetric terms and practical estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04834)
*Davide Mandelli,Emiliano Ippoliti,Charles Plate*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统计力学框架，从第一性原理推导出稀释极限下平衡结合常数K与结合/未结合态相对布居数之间的通用表达式，使体积项来源显式化并精确追踪体积约束的传播。该框架可从任意反应坐标直方图计算K，并明确热力学态边界的定义方式。在CB[7]/1-金刚烷醇和半乳糖酸/DgoT两个体系上验证，发现常用的单箱估计器相比理论正确值偏差约1 kcal/mol，该偏差源于结合态的定义方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子模拟中计算绝对结合亲和力的方法在结合态定义上存在隐含假设，导致估计器产生系统偏差；需要通过第一性原理明确结合态定义的物理依据，从而精确计算结合常数。

Method: 从统计力学第一性原理出发，推导结合常数K与结合/未结合态相对布居数间的通用表达式；将体积约束对表达式的传播效应显式化，在均匀未结合态的物理假设下精确补偿体积贡献；通过任意反应坐标直方图构建无偏估计器，并将结合态的边界锚定到实验可观测的定义上。

Result: 在CB[7]/1-金刚烷醇主客体复合物和半乳糖酸-DgoT配体-蛋白质复合物两个体系中，所提框架显示常用的单箱估计器相比理论上正确的估计偏差约1 kcal/mol，此偏差源于对结合态边界的定义方式。

Conclusion: 通过将结合态定义从隐性假设转变为受控输入（锚定于最新实验可分辨的界定），本文提供了从分子模拟中计算绝对结合亲和力的原则性路径，使结果更可复现且物理意义明确。

Abstract: We present a statistical-mechanics framework for computing equilibrium binding constants $K$ in the dilute limit. From first principles, we derive a general expression relating $K$ to the relative populations of the bound and unbound states. Its transparency has twofold advantage: it makes the origin of the unbound-state volumetric term explicit, and it allows one to track exactly how an imposed volume restraint propagates through the expression. This makes $K$ directly computable, as restrained simulations can account for the volumetric contribution exactly, under the physically mild assumption of a homogeneous unbound state. The resulting estimators are computable from histograms of any suitably defined reaction coordinate, and determine unambiguously how the boundaries of the thermodynamic states of interest must be defined. We apply our framework to the cucurbit[7]uril/1-adamantanol host--guest complex and the galactonate--DgoT ligand--protein complex. Our results show that commonly used single-bin estimators depart from the theoretically correct one by $\approx 1$~kcal/mol in both systems. This shift originates in the definition of the bound state: by anchoring that definition to what state-of-the-art experiments resolve, the theory turns it from a hidden assumption into a controlled input, and provides a principled route to absolute binding affinities from molecular simulations.

</details>

### [2] [Statistical learning theory and Occam's razor: Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04049)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论出发，为奥卡姆剃刀原则（偏好简单性）提供了一个方法论上的辩护：通过正则化在拟合度与简单性之间进行权衡，是基于理论可靠性和"所见即所得"保证的必要手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥卡姆剃刀原则在科学哲学和机器学习中都受到广泛关注，但至今缺乏一个令人信服的合理性论证。作者试图填补这一空白，从统计学习理论出发为"偏好简单性"提供严格的理由。

Method: 作者基于先前提出的"核心论证"（core argument），从统计学习理论推导出正则化程序的合理性。核心思路是采用目的-手段（means-ends）论证：为了获得理论可靠性和"所见即所得"（what-you-see-is-what-you-get）的保证，必须对简单性相对于拟合度实施某种偏好，即进行正则化。

Result: 该论证提供了一个真正的方法论辩护，既不会退化为纯粹的实用主义原则（即单纯为了简单而偏好简单），也不会依赖于本体论假设（即认为真理本身就是简单的）。

Conclusion: 正则化（以拟合度换取简单性）可以从统计学习理论中获得辩护，它确保了理论可靠性，这是一种不依赖本体论预设或纯粹审美偏好的方法论合理性。

Abstract: The principle of Occam's razor, which instructs us to prefer simplicity in inductive inference, has attracted much scrutiny both in the philosophy of science and in machine learning. In either field, however, a justification for the principle has been elusive. In this paper, building on an earlier "core argument," I spell out a justification from statistical learning theory for the procedure of regularization: for trading off fit for simplicity. The means-ends argument is that in order to profit from theoretical reliability and "what-you-see-is-what-you-get" guarantees, one must implement a certain preference for simplicity over fit. This is a genuine methodological justification, which neither collapses to a purely pragmatic principle that we prefer simplicity for its own sake, nor to an ontological assumption that the truth is simple.

</details>


### [3] [Automatic Statistical Test for Rationally Expressible Algorithms by Selective Inference, with Applications to Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04667)
*Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shuichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AutoSI是一个自动化选择性推断（Selective Inference）框架，能够自动构造选择事件并处理任意可表示为数据有理函数的算法，使精确选择性推断从仅限线性/二次不等式的窄类扩展到更广泛的算法族，且保证有限样本下的p值精确有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确选择性推断（SI）虽然能提供统计上有效的p值，但针对每个新算法都需要专家手动推导并实现"选择事件"（即假设被选中的条件），这导致精确SI长期以来只适用于少数窄类算法。现有的精确方法也仅能处理由线性或二次不等式描述的选择事件。

Method: AutoSI通过两个途径解决上述障碍：(1) 从算法的各个操作步骤自动构造选择事件，用户只需将算法写成普通的NumPy风格代码，无需手动推导；(2) 将SI可处理的选择事件类别从线性/二次不等式扩展到任何可通过数据有理函数（多项式之比）表达的算法。

Result: 作者证明了AutoSI计算出的p值在有限样本下是精确有效的，并在三个特征选择方法上进行了演示（每个方法仅需几十行代码）。其中lasso结合交叉验证R²来选择正则化参数这一方法，在现有精确SI框架中无法处理，AutoSI使其成为可能。合成数据和真实数据实验表明，p值在标称水平上控制了第一类错误率（假阳性率）并保持较高功效。

Conclusion: AutoSI通过自动化选择事件的构造并将可处理算法的范围扩展到有理函数类，显著降低了精确选择性推断的工程门槛和理论约束，使得更广泛的算法族（如lasso与交叉验证调参组合）也能享受精确有效的统计推断。

Abstract: Selective inference (SI) provides statistically valid $p$-values for hypotheses selected by applying an algorithm to the data, correcting for the bias that arises when the same data are used both to select and to test a hypothesis. Developing an SI procedure for a new algorithm, however, has required an expert to derive, and then implement, the selection event, i.e., the conditions under which the hypothesis is selected. Repeating this specialized effort for every new algorithm is why exact SI has so far been available for only a narrow class. We propose AutoSI, a framework that removes this barrier in two ways. First, AutoSI constructs the selection event automatically from the algorithm's individual operations, so the user only writes the algorithm as ordinary NumPy-like code and derives nothing by hand. Second, AutoSI broadens the class of selection events SI can handle: existing exact methods are limited to selection events characterized by linear or quadratic inequalities in the data, whereas AutoSI covers any algorithm expressible through rational functions of the data (ratios of polynomials). We prove that the $p$-values computed by AutoSI are exactly valid in finite samples. We demonstrate AutoSI on three feature-selection methods, each written in a few dozen lines of code. One of these methods, the lasso with its tuning parameter selected by cross-validated $R^2$, cannot be handled within existing exact SI frameworks and is made possible by AutoSI. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the resulting $p$-values control the type I error rate (i.e., the false positive rate) at the nominal level while retaining high power.

</details>


### [4] [Intrinsic-Hybrid Latent Diffusion Models for Generative Modeling on Unknown Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04827)
*Yizhu Wang,Mu Niu,Xiaochen Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出内在混合潜扩散模型（ILDM），将概率降维与未知流形上的几何感知扩散相结合，通过将潜空间解释为黎曼流形的坐标图，并利用概率解码器量化几何与不确定性，在混合扩散过程中根据局部不确定性在黎曼与欧几里得动力学间切换，显著提升高维数据生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型（DM）依赖大数据集且忽略内在几何结构；潜扩散模型（LDM）虽通过潜空间缓解高维问题，但强制施加欧几里得结构，难以捕捉底层流形几何，尤其在数据稀疏场景下问题尤为突出。作者旨在同时解决高维性和几何结构缺失两个局限。

Method: ILDM将潜空间视为未知黎曼流形的一个坐标图（chart），通过概率解码器量化流形几何与不确定性。前向过程是一种混合扩散机制，根据局部不确定性在黎曼动力学（由解码器导出的概率度量张量控制）与欧几里得动力学之间切换。训练采用针对混合扩散设置的近似去噪分数匹配方法，反向过程由混合朗之万动力学定义。

Result: 在COIL-100、MNIST和心脏MRI数据集上的实验表明，ILDM相较于标准扩散模型和潜扩散模型，在FID和LPIPS指标上均取得更低的分数，显著提升生成质量。

Conclusion: ILDM通过将几何感知扩散与概率降维整合，有效解决了现有扩散模型忽视流形几何结构的问题，在数据稀疏场景下展现出优越的生成性能，为混合黎曼-欧几里得生成建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce the Intrinsic Hybrid Latent Diffusion Model (ILDM), a generative framework that integrates probabilistic dimensionality reduction with geometry-aware diffusion on unknown manifolds. While diffusion models (DMs) have achieved state-of-the-art results in high-dimensional data synthesis, they rely on large training datasets and ignore intrinsic geometric structure. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) address the high dimensionality by learning a latent space, but they typically impose a Euclidean structure, failing to capture the underlying manifold geometry, especially problematic in data-sparse regimes. ILDM addresses these limitations by interpreting the latent space as a chart of an unknown Riemannian manifold, with geometry and uncertainty quantified through a probabilistic decoder. The forward process is a hybrid diffusion that switches between Riemannian and Euclidean dynamics based on local uncertainty, where the Riemannian component is governed by a probabilistic metric tensor derived from the decoder. To learn the generative dynamics, we introduce an approximate denoising score matching method tailored to the hybrid diffusion setting, enabling a backward process defined by hybrid Langevin dynamics. Experiments on COIL-100, MNIST, and cardiac MRI datasets demonstrate that ILDM significantly improves generation quality, achieving lower FID and LPIPS scores compared to standard diffusion and latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [5] [Stable Density Ridges: Consistency and Convergence of Subspace Constrained Mean Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05112)
*Wanli Qiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS)算法的理论收敛目标并非传统上认为的"静态脊线"，而是本文提出的"稳定脊线"这一新型几何结构。作者基于动力系统视角重新定义了SCMS的理论收敛目标，并开发了广义SCMS框架，证明了其均匀R-线性收敛性、拓扑满射性、Hausdorff距离下的估计收敛速率，同时指出了原始SCMS算法的多项式时间复杂度问题并提供了更高效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为SCMS算法轨迹收敛于经典密度脊线（静态脊线，通过密度梯度和Hessian的特征值/特征向量定义），但本文证明这一假设在一般情况下并不成立。根本原因在于静态脊线的定义未考虑底层向量场连续流动过程中尾随特征空间的旋转，导致SCMS算法的真实收敛目标与理论设定不一致。

Method: 本文提出"稳定脊线"概念，从动力系统视角并利用投影密度梯度的Jacobian矩阵来定义这一新几何结构。基于此构建了采用恒定步长的广义SCMS框架，并对其进行严格的收敛性分析，包括均匀R-线性收敛性证明和到稳定脊线的拓扑满射性证明，同时推导了Hausdorff距离下稳定脊线估计的收敛速率。

Result: 证明了稳定脊线才是SCMS算法真正的理论收敛目标；广义SCMS框架实现了均匀R-线性收敛并具有到稳定脊线的拓扑满射性；获得了基于Hausdorff距离的稳定脊线估计收敛速率；揭示了原始SCMS因Mean Shift算子将步长与平滑带宽隐式耦合而导致多项式时间计算复杂度的缺陷，而广义框架提供了统计一致且更高效的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入"稳定脊线"这一动力学视角下的新几何概念，纠正了SCMS算法理论收敛目标的重要误解，并提供了一个统计一致、计算高效的广义SCMS框架，为密度脊线提取领域奠定了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS) algorithm is a popular nonparametric method for extracting density ridges, which serve as a low-dimensional representation of high-dimensional data. It is a widely held belief in the literature that SCMS trajectories converge to the classical density ridge, which we call the "static ridge", defined via the density gradient and the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the density's Hessian. In this paper, we demonstrate that this assumption does not hold in general, as the static definition fails to account for the rotation of the trailing eigenspace along the continuous flow of the algorithm's underlying vector field. To resolve this, we propose a paradigm shift by introducing the "stable ridge", a novel geometric structure defined through the lens of dynamical systems and the Jacobian of the projected density gradient. We prove that this stable ridge is the true theoretical target of the SCMS algorithm. Building upon this foundation, we develop a generalized SCMS framework utilizing a constant step size, establishing its uniform R-linear convergence and topological surjectivity onto the stable ridge. We further derive the rates of convergence for estimating the stable ridge in terms of the Hausdorff distance. Finally, we expose that the original SCMS algorithm suffers from polynomial-time computational complexity, which is caused by implicitly coupling the step size to the smoothing bandwidth via the Mean Shift operator, and demonstrate how our generalized framework provides a statistically consistent and more efficient solution.

</details>

### [6] [Iwahori Fundamental Local Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04207)
*Taeuk Nam*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了三个温和分歧的因子化模范畴的局部等价：点处的因子化Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov等价、点处的因子化Bezrukavnikov等价，以及Iwahori分歧版本的因子化基本局部等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在将已知的非分歧(温和分歧)情形下的局部等价推广到因子化范畴的框架中，并将Iwahori分歧情形纳入统一的理论体系，从而建立更广泛的几何表示论等价。

Method: 作者构造了三个具体的等价：首先构造因子化版本的Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov等价（在一点处），其次构造因子化版本的Bezrukavnikov等价（在一点处），最后构造Iwahori-ramified版本的因子化基本局部等价。

Result: 成功构造了三个温和分歧的因子化模范畴的局部等价，将经典的Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov等价、Bezrukavnikov等价以及基本局部等价推广到因子化设定，并引入了Iwahori分歧版本。

Conclusion: 这些因子化版本的局部等价为几何表示论中的基本等价关系提供了更一般的框架，特别是涵盖了Iwahori分歧情形的因子化基本局部等价，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct three tamely ramified local equivalences of factorization module categories. The first is a factorization version of the Arkhipov-Bezrukavnikov equivalence at a point. The second is a factorization version of the Bezrukavnikov equivalence at a point. The third is an Iwahori-ramified version of the factorizable Fundamental Local Equivalence.

</details>


### [7] [Smooth affine surfaces properly dominated by $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04214)
*Buddhadev Hajra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对接受来自 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 的有限满射态射的平滑复仿射曲面进行了完全分类。利用对数 Kodaira 维数理论，证明了这类曲面的对数 Kodaira 维数只能是 $-\infty$ 或 $0$，并确定所有可能性：维数为 $-\infty$ 的仅有 $\mathbf{C}^2$ 和 $\mathbf{C}\times\mathbf{C}^*$；维数为 $0$ 的恰为 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 和 Fujita 曲面 $H[-1,0,-1]$，从而验证了 M. Furushima 在 1989 年预言的分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是对被 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 适当支配（即存在有限满射态射）的平滑复仿射曲面进行分类。这一问题由 M. Furushima 在 1989 年提出预期分类，此前尚未得到完整严格的证明。通过建立这一分类，可以更深入地理解代数簇之间的态射结构以及仿射曲面的几何性质。

Method: 本文采用按对数 Kodaira 维数分类平滑仿射曲面的经典分类理论作为主要工具。首先利用有限态射与 Kodaira 维数在覆盖下的行为关系，将问题分解为对数 Kodaira 维数为 $-\infty$ 和 $0$ 两种情形分别处理，然后逐一排除其他可能性并确定每种维数下所有可能的曲面类型。

Result: 主要结果显示：被 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 有限满射支配的平滑仿射曲面的对数 Kodaira 维数只能是 $-\infty$ 或 $0$。具体地，维数为 $-\infty$ 的曲面只有 $\mathbf{C}^2$ 和 $\mathbf{C}\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 两种；维数为 $0$ 的曲面恰为 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 本身和 Fujita 曲面 $H[-1,0,-1]$。

Conclusion: 本文完整确定了平滑复仿射曲面中被 $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ 适当支配的全部类别，共计四种曲面，严格验证了 Furushima 在 1989 年提出的预期分类。该结果充实了仿射曲面分类理论，并为研究代数曲面上的有限态射提供了新的分类依据。

Abstract: We classify smooth complex affine surfaces admitting a finite surjective morphism from $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$. Using the classification theory of smooth affine surfaces according to logarithmic Kodaira dimension, we show that every such surface has logarithmic Kodaira dimension either $-\infty$ or $0$, and determine all possibilities. More precisely, the only surfaces of logarithmic Kodaira dimension $-\infty$ are $\mathbf{C}^2$ and $\mathbf{C}\times\mathbf{C}^*$, while those of logarithmic Kodaira dimension $0$ are precisely $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ and Fujita's surface $H[-1,0,-1]$. This establishes the classification of smooth affine surfaces properly dominated by $\mathbf{C}^*\times\mathbf{C}^*$ anticipated by M.~Furushima in 1989.

</details>


### [8] [Prym--Tyurin varieties coming from intermediate coverings of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04298)
*Víctor Valdebenito Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全新的几何构造，从光滑曲线的Zp×Zp伽罗华覆盖的中间覆盖中构造奇素数指数p的Prym-Tyurin簇，并精确确定了何时该构造成立（基曲线为椭圆曲线或亏格2曲线的各向同性étale覆盖），进而证明了相应的Prym-Tyurin映射是一般有限的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prym-Tyurin簇是Prym簇的推广，在代数几何和模空间理论中具有重要意义。已有的构造方法较为有限，本文旨在通过Zp×Zp伽罗华覆盖的中间覆盖这一新的几何构造途径，扩展Prym-Tyurin簇的构造来源，并研究其映射性质。

Following Joyce's recipe [Joy21], we introduce Joyce's enumerative invariant for the Quot scheme $\text{Quot}_{r,d}(E)$. The invariant can be viewed as a generalization of the virtual fundamental cycle of the Quot scheme. We evaluate intersection pairings on the Quot scheme $\text{Quot}_{\text{rank}(E)-1,d}(E)$ by computing its invariant explicitly. Following the reformulation of sheaf-theoretic Virasoro constraints in terms of Joyce's vertex algebra framework in [BLM24], we give a proof of the Virasoro constraints for the Quot scheme $\text{Quot}_{r,d}(E)$. Abstract: We obtain a motivic and a cohomological McKay correspondence for finite linearly reductive group schemes in arbitrary characteristic. In particular, we prove that if $V$ is a finite dimensional vector space and $G$ is a finite linearly reductive subgroup scheme of $\mathrm{SL}(V)$, then the Euler number of any crepant resolution of $V/G$ is equal to the number of irreducible algebraic representations of $G$. We obtain these McKay correspondences as a consequence of a motivic change of variables formula applied to $[V/G] \to V/G$. If $G$ is non-reduced, as can happen in positive characteristic, the stack quotient $[V/G]$ is not Deligne-Mumford. Therefore in order to prove this change of variables formula and the resulting McKay correspondences, we generalize the authors' theory of motivic integration for Artin stacks to arbitrary characteristic, which may be of independent interest.

### [17] [A Primal Perspective on Distributionally Robust Optimization: An Investigation on Modeling and Solution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04123)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Evacuation planning for disaster preparedness requires making critical decisions under uncertainty before the number and spatial distribution of evacuees are known, including shelter location, evacuation route assignment, and relief supply prepositioning. Unlike static planning models, which fix routes before demand is known, and reactive heuristics, which respond only to the current backlog, our approach anticipates future arrivals when weighing immediate collection against consolidation. We formulate the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a neural approximate dynamic programming framework for centralized dispatch. The method introduces a dual value function decomposition that separately represents vehicle states and collection-centre states through neural networks trained on post-decision states. These learned estimates are integrated through a matching formulation that selects dispatch actions while balancing supply availability, demand urgency, and downstream opportunity cost. Computational experiments on a realistic Greater Toronto Area network compare the proposed policy with myopic baselines and ablation variants. Results show that the proposed dual value function policy raises the share of sample volume delivered on time by 1 to 9 percentage points over myopic baselines, with the largest gains under tight fleet, capacity, deadline, and routing constraints; these on-time gains, in turn, reduce reliance on costly external couriers.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出了"方向扰动经验优化"(EO+)的统一框架，将多种数据驱动优化方法统一表述为对经验优化目标函数进行方向性扰动的形式。在此基础上，作者从理论上分析EO+方法的统计性能：一方面证明在缺乏正确指定侧信息时最多只有二阶改进；另一方面证明在利用几何有效侧信息时可以实现一阶改进，并通过超额风险估计（基于系统知识或bootstrap重采样）来指导超参数选择以最大化一阶增益。

Result: 主要理论结果为：(1) 无侧信息情况下EO+方法最多实现二阶改进，即"没有免费午餐"；(2) 利用几何有效的侧信息并选择显著大于文献推荐值的超参数时，可以实现一阶改进；(3) 提出的超额风险估计方法能够有效最大化一阶增益；(4) 该增益与控制变量原则（蒙特卡洛模拟中的方差缩减技术）存在深刻联系，解释了为何几何有效的侧信息是必要的。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的理论框架来理解数据驱动优化方法的统计性能边界。核心结论是：不充分利用额外侧信息的数据驱动优化方法在统计性能上存在固有局限（至多二阶改进），而正确利用几何有效侧信息并配合大规模超参数选择，可以实现实质性的一阶统计改进。这一发现对实践中的方法选择和超参数调整具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Recent proliferation of data-optimization integration has led to a range of methods that aim to improve the statistical performance of data-driven optimization decisions. However, while many of these methods are motivated intuitively from a robustness or regularization perspective, their resulting statistical benefits are often unclear and, even if available, are established on a case-by-case basis. We provide a systematic dissection of data-driven optimization formulations using the view of "directionally perturbed" empirical optimization (EO). Specifically, this umbrella of formulations, which we call "EO+", covers many existing data-driven optimization methods, including regularization, distributionally robust optimization, transfer learning, and analogous methods for contextual optimization. On the one hand, we argue that without additional, correctly specified, side information, any EO+ method can result in at most second-order improvements. This provides a negative conclusion, namely ``no free lunch is possible", on the statistical power of EO+. On the other hand, we show that when leveraging side information that is geometrically effective, achieving first-order improvements is possible by choosing hyperparameters that are significantly larger than what is typically suggested in the literature. Moreover, we construct a principled methodology based on excess risk estimation, via either system knowledge or bootstrap resampling, to maximize the first-order gain. We demonstrate how this gain connects to the control-variate principle, a variance reduction technique in the Monte Carlo simulation literature, which helps explain why geometrically effective side information is necessary.

Abstract: We consider minimizing a smooth, strongly convex function over a convex set. Projected gradient descent is known to converge linearly in this setting, but each iteration requires a projection onto the feasible set, which may be computationally expensive.
  We show that when the feasible set is smooth, projection can be replaced by one gradient computation and a single supporting-tangent computation per iteration, while preserving linear convergence. Moreover, the required tangent can be approximated to sufficient accuracy using $\widetilde O(d)$ membership-oracle queries, where $d$ is the ambient dimension. Previously, projection-free linear convergence was known only for polyhedral sets or for sets that are both smooth and strongly convex.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies $N$-player stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) differential games from the perspective of $α$-potential games. We first consider a closed-loop LQ game with multiplicative noise, where both the drift and the diffusion coefficients depend linearly on the state and the full control vector. For this model, we derive probabilistic and partial differential equation (PDE) representations for the first- and second-order linear derivatives of the players' cost function and prove the equivalence between them. We then develop an open-loop stochastic LQ \(α\)-potential game framework. Using the linear derivative construction, we build an \(α\)-potential function and derive an explicit upper bound for the approximation parameter \(α\) in terms of the model coefficients and the admissible control radius. Moreover, the minimization of the \(α\)-potential function is reduced to a finite-dimensional stochastic control problem by augmenting the state with the variational process, which yields an open-loop \(α\)-Nash equilibrium. As an application, we revisit a network LQ game considered in \cite{GuoLiZhang2025} and show that the feedback representation obtained from our approach coincides with the feedback in the existing conditional McKean--Vlasov approach, while our characterization follows directly from a standard finite-dimensional LQ control problem.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于异构分布式系统的Grassmannian增量聚合方法，用于有限和特征空间（eigenspace）计算。该方法仅刷新到达的组件并复用缓存的梯度，避免了全局同步和并行传输，通过外在极坐标更新保持子空间的几何结构。理论分析建立了目标函数的紧致角度依赖梯度优势特征和盆地不变性，证明了包括宽盆地和局部锐化区域的两阶段线性收敛。实验表明在串行和分布式PCA上优于代表性基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We propose MELMO (Moreau Envelope Smoothing with Linear Minimization Oracles), an algorithm for composite optimization problems of the form min x f (x) + g(T x), where f is smooth and g may be non-smooth. The method leverages the Moreau envelope to smooth the non-smooth component while adapting to problem geometry through linear minimization oracles. Assuming g is $ρ$-weakly convex, we establish a family of convergence bounds parameterized by the step-size and smoothing schedules, thereby making explicit the trade-off between optimizing the smoothed objective and recovering stationarity for the original composite problem. In particular, one regime yields O(k -1/4 ) rates for both the smoothed-gradient norm and a composite stationarity proxy, while another yields O(k -1/3 ) for the smoothed-gradient norm together with O(k -1/4 ) for the composite proxy. We also establish a K-horizon-dependent convergence rate that yields O(K -1/3 ) for the composite proxy. Empirically, MELMO is competitive with variable smoothing and subgradient baselines on sparse low-rank matrix factorization and image denoising.

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization methods are the standard instruments for the training of deep neural networks (DNNs). In many relevant artificial intelligence (AI) systems - such as popular large language models (LLMs)-not the standard SGD scheme is used as the optimization method but instead suitable accelerated variants of SGD are employed. One of the most popular methods of such accelerated SGD variants is the momentum orthogonalized by Newton-Schulz (MUON) optimizer proposed by Jordan et al. in 2024. The MUON optimizer exploits the special matrix structure of the weight parameters in the training of the DNNs and, in its original form, employs five Newton-Schultz (NS) matrix steps in each MUON iteration.

The considered optimizer covers MUON with the original NS polynomial as well as MUON combined with the recently proposed Polar Express method as special cases. For a simple class of stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) we show for almost every mini-batch size that MUON fails to converge to the solution of the SOP as the number of gradient steps converges to infinity. We also establish an error analysis for MUON with the generalized NS steps that provides convergence rates in terms of the number of gradient steps and in terms of the size of the mini-batch. We illustrate our general error analysis for MUON in the case of several concrete examples including quadratic stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) as well as $\ell_2$ regularized logistic regression for binary classification.

The proposed methodology was compared against an integrated mixed-integer linear programming baseline adapted from the literature under realistic operational disruptions, including failed patient confirmations and surgery suspensions. Results show that the proposed framework achieves a more balanced distribution between offered and demanded surgical time across specialties while maintaining high operating-room utilization and substantially lower computational times than the integrated baseline. In the 20-week scenario, the proposed methodology achieved lower waiting times and operated a larger number of patients while preserving operational flexibility. TL;DR: 本文针对带Shannon熵核的Bregman投影梯度法(BPGM)的迭代收敛性这一长期开放问题，提出了新的收敛分析框架。该框架的核心是提出"缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz(SKŁ)性质"这一新概念，证明了该性质能保证BPGM的迭代收敛，且对所有连续次解析函数成立。进一步证明了当SKŁ指数为1/2时，BPGM序列线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPGM的迭代收敛性尤其是带Shannon熵核的情况一直是长期未解决的开放问题。已有的收敛结果往往依赖于核梯度的Lipschitz连续性或对目标函数的严格限制条件，适用范围有限。因此需要开发一种不依赖这些限制性条件的新收敛分析框架。

Method: 提出"缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz(SKŁ)性质"这一新概念，捕获函数在Bregman几何下的局部增长行为。在此基础上建立BPGM的收敛分析框架：(1)证明SKŁ性质保证BPGM迭代收敛且对所有连续次解析函数成立；(2)证明当问题具有SKŁ指数1/2时，BPGM序列线性收敛；(3)通过严格互补性和目标函数梯度的局部Lipschitz连续性，证明KŁ指数1/2可推出SKŁ指数1/2。

Result: 证明了SKŁ性质确保BPGM在线性约束下、针对广泛目标函数类的强收敛结果；证明了SKŁ性质对所有连续次解析函数成立；证明了SKŁ指数1/2保证BPGM线性收敛，并给出了SKŁ指数1/2的具体例证条件。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入SKŁ性质，为BPGM迭代收敛这一开放问题迈出了第一步解决的关键步伐，建立了一个不依赖于核梯度Lipschitz连续性的全新收敛分析框架，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The iterate convergence of \textit{Bregman projected gradient method} (BPGM) has remained a long-standing open problem, especially for the widely adopted Shannon entropy kernel. Existing convergence results are often limited, relying on Lipschitz continuity of the kernel's gradient or restrictive conditions on the objective function. In this paper, we develop a novel convergence analysis framework for the BPGM with the Shannon entropy kernel, yielding strong convergence results for a broad class of objective functions under linear constraints. The cornerstone of our framework is a new concept called \textit{scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz} (SKŁ) property, which captures the local growth behavior of a function under the Bregman geometry. We show that the SKŁ property ensures the iterate convergence of BPGM and holds for all continuous subanalytical functions. Furthermore, we prove that the BPGM sequence exhibits linear convergence if the problem possesses an SKŁ exponent of $1/2$. We then furnish the examples of functions with the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ by proving that the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ is implied by the KŁ exponent $1/2$ under strict complementarity and local Lipschitz continuity of the objective's gradient. Building on these novel results, our work takes a first step towards resolving the open problem of BPGM iterate convergence.

</details>

Abstract: Effects of wind direction in realistic urban canopies remain difficult to characterize systematically because local flow patterns, building-height variability, and turbulent statistics within a realistic urban canopy may respond differently to changes in the approaching wind. To investigate these multi-scale directional impacts, we conducted high-resolution large-eddy simulations of the atmospheric boundary layer over the Zona Universitaria Pedralbes district in Barcelona. The computational meshes employ fourth-order spectral elements, yielding pedestrian-level resolutions below 1 m and resulting in approximately 5.0e8 degrees of freedom. Sixteen wind directions uniformly distributed over 360deg are simulated to capture the full directional response of the urban fabric.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用气固两相"尘埃气体"框架，均匀球形颗粒通过Stokes阻力与不可压缩牛顿流体耦合。在正弦压力梯度驱动的时周期基态流动上线性化控制方程，运用Floquet分析研究时间周期扰动的不稳定性，系统考察Reynolds数、Womersley数、脉动幅度、颗粒弛豫时间和颗粒质量分数的影响。

Result: 稳态极限下，极短弛豫时间颗粒促进失稳，而有限弛豫时间通过相间滑移和阻力介导的阻尼使扰动稳定。脉动条件下，增益脉动幅度在低Womersley数下失稳，在高Womersley数下稳定，此行为由振荡穿透深度主导，颗粒弛豫时间和质量加载通过相间动量交换系统性偏移这一转变点。临界对应值始终较小，表明颗粒-流体强耦合，排除了共振型颗粒动力学。

Conclusion: 该研究为脉动颗粒两相流的稳定性提供了统一物理框架，对理解生理流动及周期性驱动的多相系统中的不稳定性机制具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We investigate the linear stability of particle-laden pulsatile channel flow using Floquet analysis within a two-phase dusty-gas framework. Uniformly distributed spherical particles are coupled to an incompressible Newtonian fluid through Stokes drag, and the governing equations are linearized about a time-periodic base flow driven by a sinusoidally varying pressure gradient. The effects of Reynolds number, Womersley number, pulsation amplitude, particle relaxation time, and particle mass fraction on temporal instability are examined. In the steady limit, particles with very short relaxation times destabilize the flow, whereas finite relaxation times introduce interphase slip and drag-mediated damping that stabilize disturbances. Under pulsatile forcing, increasing pulsation amplitude destabilizes the flow at low Womersley numbers but stabilizes it at sufficiently high Womersley numbers. This transition is governed by the penetration depth of oscillatory motion and is systematically shifted by particle relaxation time and mass loading through interphase momentum exchange. A critical corresponding value remains small throughout, indicating strong particle-fluid coupling and ruling out resonance-like particle dynamics. These findings provide a unified physical framework for the stability of pulsatile particle-laden flows relevant to physiological and periodically forced multiphase systems.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种用于非定常二维不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的人工压缩（AC）物理信息神经网络（PINN），用标量参数ε控制的松弛项替代刚性的无散约束。研究表明ε越大，残差散度越大、速度误差越高，且随ε减小两者单调下降并趋于饱和。在Re=100圆柱尾流中，纯前向AC-PINN会坍缩到稳态对称分支，无法再现von Kármán涡街；但通过同化来自边界层解析有限元参考解的数百个稀疏速度传感器数据，可在尾流区恢复非定常涡街至7%误差，脱落频率与参考解偏差在3%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN在处理不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程时面临刚性无散约束这一微分-代数约束带来的挑战，难以直接最小化。作者引入人工压缩松弛方法，通过单个标量参数ε将刚性约束转化为PINN可直接优化的普通残差，旨在简化约束处理并评估该方法在非定常流动中的可行性。

Method: 采用人工压缩（AC）松弛替代刚性无散约束，引入压力时间导数将微分-代数约束转化为普通残差；在Taylor-Green涡上量化ε的影响；对Re=100圆柱尾流，对比纯前向AC-PINN与同化数百个稀疏速度传感器数据的AC-PINN；参考解为边界层解析的有限元解，并对其Strouhal数进行验证。

Result: Taylor-Green涡上验证了ε与残差散度及速度误差的定量关系（残差散度∝ε|∂t p|）；圆柱尾流中纯前向AC-PINN无法捕捉非定常涡脱落，而同化稀疏传感器数据后可恢复涡街（7%误差）及脱落频率（3%以内）；有限元参考解的Strouhal数为0.176，经边界层解析后接近文献值0.164-0.172但略高。

Conclusion: 人工压缩松弛使PINN能直接处理不可压缩NS方程的无散约束，且参数ε可控制约束满足精度；然而纯前向AC-PINN在圆柱绕流中缺乏捕捉非定常失稳的能力，必须借助数据同化才能恢复von Kármán涡街；同时需注意验证基准自身的保真度限制了结果的独立有效性。

Abstract: We study a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for the unsteady, two-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations in which the stiff divergence-free constraint is replaced by an artificial-compressibility (AC) relaxation governed by a single scalar parameter $\eps$. The relaxation reintroduces a pressure time derivative, converting a differential-algebraic constraint into an ordinary residual that a PINN can minimise directly. On the Taylor--Green vortex, which admits a closed-form unsteady solution, we quantify the effect of $\eps$: the residual divergence scales as $\eps\,|\partial_t p|$, so larger $\eps$ raises both the divergence and the velocity error, and both decrease monotonically and saturate as $\eps$ is reduced. On the $Re=100$ cylinder wake the plain forward AC-PINN collapses to the steady symmetric branch and does not reproduce von Kármán shedding; assimilating a few hundred sparse velocity sensors from a boundary-layer-resolved finite-element reference (whose Strouhal number, $0.176$, we bring close to the $0.164$--$0.172$ literature band by resolving the separating shear layer, though it remains just above it) recovers the unsteady vortex street to $7\%$ over the wake and its shedding frequency to within $3\%$ of that same reference --- a bound set by the reference's own fidelity rather than an independent validation against the true flow.

</details>


### [36] [TIDE: A Physically Diverse 3D Turbulence Benchmark Dataset for Advancing Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04222)
*Yilong Dai,Yiming Sun,Yiheng Chen,Shengyu Chen,Peyman Givi,Xiaowei Jia,Runlong Yu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE，一个256^3分辨率的3D不可压缩湍流直接数值模拟（DNS）语料库与基准测试，包含15种配置、独立系综、压力场及方程级验证，并设计了五个任务、标准化基线和物理保真度指标，揭示当前学习模型在精度、物理保真度和条件变量建模三方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流是物理动力学机器学习的关键测试平台，其控制方程精确已知。然而现有研究多在2D进行，3D湍流物理本质不同且模拟成本高昂；同时现有3D资源通常每个配置仅提供一个实现，难以区分模型是学到了动力学规律还是仅拟合了单一流场的统计特征。

Method: TIDE提供了256^3分辨率的3D不可压缩湍流DNS语料库，包含15种配置、八个受控变化轴、独立系综、压力场和方程级验证。基准测试涵盖五个任务、标准化学习基线、受控泛化划分，并结合逐点误差与物理保真度指标进行评估。

Result: 在主要预报配置下，当前学习模型仅勉强优于持久性预测，且误差约为已知真实方程的谱求解器误差的两倍。更低的逐点误差可能伴随严重失真的小尺度动力学，说明精度本身不能保证物理保真度。泛化实验显示大多数曲线偏移反映了训练覆盖范围有限，而从受迫到衰减的迁移揭示了缺失的条件变量：在受迫下训练的算子在外力移除后仍预测驱动演化。

Conclusion: TIDE使精度、保真度和条件变量建模三大核心差距变得可量化衡量，解决这些差距是未来研究的中心开放问题。

Abstract: Turbulence is a central testbed for machine learning on physical dynamics because its governing laws are known exactly. However, most existing studies remain in 2D, while 3D turbulence has fundamentally different physics and is far more costly to simulate. Existing 3D resources also typically provide only one realization per configuration, making it difficult to distinguish learning the dynamics from fitting the statistics of a single flow. In this paper, we introduce TIDE (Turbulent Incompressible DNS Ensembles), a 256^3 DNS corpus and benchmark for 3D incompressible turbulence, with 15 configurations on eight controlled axes, independent ensembles, pressure fields, and equation-level verification. The benchmark includes five tasks, standardized learned baselines, controlled generalization splits, and physical-fidelity metrics alongside pointwise error. Across the main forecasting configurations, current learned models barely outperform persistence and still make about twice the error of a spectral solver given the true equations. Moreover, lower pointwise error can coincide with severely distorted small-scale dynamics, showing that accuracy alone does not ensure physical fidelity. Generalization results further show that most regime shifts reflect limited training coverage, whereas forced-to-decay transfer exposes a missing conditioning variable: operators trained under forcing continue to predict driven evolution when the external drive is removed. Closing these accuracy, fidelity, and conditioning gaps is the central open problem made measurable by TIDE.

</details>

Abstract: Fog harvesting efficiency with mesh collectors is governed by complex interactions between droplet inertia and geometry-induced flow structures. Although previous studies have primarily relied on mean-flow metrics, the present work introduces a spectral-dynamic framework to examine an important but often overlooked control on droplet capture. A two-way coupled Eulerian-Lagrangian model is used to simulate droplet-laden flow (2-40 microm) across five representative mesh geometries. The results show that capture efficiency correlates not only with the magnitude of velocity fluctuations, but also with their spectral distribution and persistence. Frequency-domain analysis indicates that mesh geometry redistributes fluctuation energy across pore and obstruction regions, thereby defining a characteristic flow timescale. By comparing this flow timescale with the droplet response time, a dynamic matching parameter, Π= droplet response time/flow time scale, is introduced. The highest capture efficiency occurs when Π is order unity, corresponding to sustained droplet-flow interaction in the near-mesh region. Geometries that generate broadband, moderately amplified spectral content (e.g., triangular mesh) increase droplet residence time and interception probability, whereas geometries with either weak or highly localized fluctuations reduce performance through insufficient forcing or premature bypass. A physics-inspired correlation for capture efficiency is proposed based on this condition. The study therefore provides mechanistic design guidance, rather than a definitive optimum, for geometry optimization in fog-harvesting meshes.

</details>


### [38] [Expansion of a hole in a viscoelastic liquid sheet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04345)
*Tachin Ruangkriengsin,Rodolfo Brandão,Howard A. Stone*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文分析了粘弹性液体薄膜中孔洞的指数膨胀行为。通过Oldroyd-B模型，推导了扩展薄膜方程和尖端区域的渐近力平衡边界条件，发现弱粘弹性（Wi≪1）和超稀极限（μp≪μs）下孔半径分别以e^(0.5+αWiβp)T和e^(0.5+α*βp)T指数增长。粘弹性应力通过方位角拉伸和径向压缩增强向外径向拉伸流动，从而提高增长率并导致膜厚变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现高粘性聚合物薄膜中刺破孔洞以指数形式膨胀，且边缘无液体积累，这与经典Taylor-Culick描述（恒速移动、液体在边缘堆积）不符。尽管该差异最初归因于粘弹性，但后续纯粘性理论也能解释，粘弹性应力的作用仍悬而未决。作者旨在明确粘弹性应力在此过程中的确切角色。

Method: 采用Oldroyd-B粘弹性模型分析自由悬浮液体薄膜中轴对称孔洞的膨胀。利用孔半径与膜厚之间的尺度分离，在孔尺度推导扩展薄膜方程，并通过尖端区域的渐近力平衡推导有效边界条件。分别求解弱粘弹性（Wi≪1）和超稀极限（μp≪μs）下的解析解。

Result: 弱粘弹性下，无量纲孔半径近似为e^(0.5+αWiβp)T，其中α≈0.224；超稀极限下近似为e^(0.5+α*βp)T（α*>0由数值计算获得）。两种机制下，粘弹性应力均提高指数增长率（相对牛顿极限），并诱导膜厚变化，边缘附近出现增厚。加速源于聚合物方位角拉伸和径向压缩引起的应力重分布，改变尖端应力平衡，产生更强的向外径向拉伸流。

Conclusion: 粘弹性应力在聚合物薄膜孔洞膨胀中起着实质性加速作用，其机制是通过聚合物链的方位角拉伸和径向压缩重新分配薄膜内应力，改变尖端力平衡，从而增强径向外流。该研究为粘弹性液体薄膜中孔洞动力学的理论理解提供了精确的解析框架。

Abstract: Experiments on highly viscous polymeric films show that punctured holes expand exponentially in time, without sustained accumulation of liquid near the rim. This response departs from the Taylor--Culick description, in which displaced liquid accumulates in a growing rim that moves at constant speed. Although these differences were initially attributed to viscoelasticity, they were later rationalized using a purely viscous theory, leaving the role of viscoelastic stresses unresolved. Using direct numerical simulations of pipe flow at $Re = 2400, 2450, 2500, 2550$, we provide evidence that these laminar gaps form a distinct dynamical state analogous to puffs: a traveling laminar pocket in a turbulent surrounding, stabilized by a shear-dependent self-tuning mechanism. Conclusion: 将基于物理的模态表示与扩散生成重建相结合，是兼顾效率与保真度的高效湍流流动预测途径。

Abstract: Forecasting turbulent flow dynamics requires a balance between predictive fidelity and computational efficiency. Diffusion-based generative models can represent complex spatiotemporal dynamics, but their application to high-dimensional turbulent flows remains computationally expensive. In contrast, proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) provides compact, physically interpretable reduced-order representations, although aggressive modal truncation can remove relevant flow structures. This work introduces a hybrid reduced-order generative forecasting framework that combines POD with Generative Learning of Effective Dynamics (G-LED). The method performs temporal prediction in a physics-based modal space and uses diffusion-based reconstruction to recover physically meaningful flow-field representations. It is assessed using experimental measurements of the turbulent wake behind a circular cylinder. Three configurations are compared: full-field G-LED, global POD-G-LED, and localized POD-G-LED. Full-field G-LED provides the highest fidelity, preserving richer vorticity fluctuations and more consistent turbulent kinetic energy distributions, but requires approximately 17 h for diffusion-model training, 7 h for Transformer training, and 3 min to predict 100 future snapshots. By transferring prediction to a reduced POD space, global POD-G-LED reduces these costs to approximately 8 h, 2 h, and 50 s, respectively, while retaining dominant wake organization and coherent energetic structures. A localized POD-G-LED formulation assigns different modal resolutions to distinct wake regions and improves vorticity statistics and energetic distributions relative to the global reduced-order configuration. These results show that coupling physics-based modal representations with diffusion-based generative reconstruction offers an effective route to efficient turbulent-flow forecasting.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 文章综述了该研究计划已成功产生尖锐上界及对应饱和极端流动的问题，并给出了一系列开放问题和可能的方法改进方向的讨论。

Abstract: Extreme flows realize the largest possible growth, either instantaneously or in finite time, of certain quantities of interest which is achieved by a suitable choice of the initial condition or the applied forcing. The quantities of interest usually measure some small-scale properties and therefore provide information about the regularity of the flow. Extreme behavior is at the heart of several open problems in fluid mechanics including the dissipation anomaly in turbulence and formation of singularities in various models of fluid flow. In this essay we describe a framework making it possible to study such extreme behavior systematically by combining mathematical analysis, scientific computation and physics. As a first step, one aims to deduce rigorous upper bounds on the growth of the quantities of interest in the solutions of a given model. These inequalities express fundamental limitations on the most extreme behavior possible among {\em all} admissible solutions. However, given how they are obtained, these bounds may be conservative and overestimate the growth actually realizable in the system. In order to probe this possibility, as the next step, we set up variational optimization problems where the growth of the quantity of interest is maximized under suitable constraints. Solution of such problems is enabled by modern methods of numerical optimization. When properties of the thus obtained maximizers match the bounds, the bounds are declared sharp and therefore cannot be fundamentally improved. Finally, properties of the solutions saturating the bounds reveal insights about the physical mechanisms realizing the extreme behavior. We survey problems where this research program has produced sharp bounds together with extreme flows saturating these bounds. A collection of open problems is then presented and we close the essay with a discussion of possible methodological improvements.

</details>

Abstract: Matrix-valued kernels provide a flexible framework for approximating vector fields from scattered data, especially when structural constraints such as divergence-free or curl-free conditions must be preserved. Another main contribution of the paper is a sharp error analysis for the corresponding kernel matrix-valued interpolation problem. We derive direct Sobolev error estimates that allow fractional regularity of the target field, and we establish Bernstein-type inequalities for the associated kernel trial spaces. These results lead to a complete inverse theorem. We also investigate stability by proving lower bounds for the smallest eigenvalues of the interpolation matrices. Numerical experiments are included to verify the theoretical results.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Learned spectral models fail at symmetry forced degenerate sectors for two distinct reasons. Where symmetry forces levels to coincide exactly, the per level observable one would normally fit is not well defined, since every unit vector of that shared space is an eigenvector; and near a symmetry protected crossing, the eigenvector observable gradient carries a factor 1/(lambda_i - lambda_j) that is genuinely singular as the gap closes. The usual response is to regularize the divergence or threshold the gap, and both carry a real cost: a fixed gap cannot both protect a forced multiplet and keep two genuinely distinct levels apart. We show a different fix, on synthetic operator families with a known symmetry answer key. A gate reads the symmetry structure directly from the observed operators, as the linear commutant of the family, one singular value decomposition nullspace, following the simultaneous block diagonalization of Maehara and Murota. Block identity is read from the centre of that commutant rather than from eigenvalue clustering, which makes the forced versus accidental distinction structural rather than metric, and the objective switches between a projector trace through forced blocks and a per level target elsewhere. Under operator estimation noise the gate classifies correctly to epsilon of about 0.3, where energy clustering already fails by 0.02. Gated fitting reaches the truth at machine precision in both the symmetric and the symmetry breaking regime, in the latter converging to a truth that is a singularity of the ungated objective, and observable bias sits at the noise floor. Result: 在多种基准测试（包括单一和耦合的慢-快Van der Pol振荡器以及Lorenz吸引子）中，AMF持续优于各种基线滤波器。并行实现方案允许以实际可行的计算成本实现高保真PDF估计。

Conclusion: AMF为非线性动力系统中的非高斯PDF估计提供了一种准确且计算高效的解决方案，通过方差保证的分裂机制与自适应分量管理，在多种基准测试中优于现有方法，且并行实现使其具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Filtering combines model predictions with measurements to estimate the probability density function (PDF) of a system state over time. The PDF often becomes highly asymmetric and even multimodal in nonlinear systems with oscillatory or chaotic dynamics. Such non-Gaussian features violate the single-Gaussian assumption underlying Kalman-type filters. To address this problem, Gaussian mixture filtering has been proposed. However, accurately propagating mixture components and adaptively adjusting their number and weights over time remain open challenges. Here, we develop an adaptive split-combine Gaussian mixture filter (AMF) that estimates the time evolution of asymmetric and multimodal PDFs by adaptively splitting and combining Gaussian particles without auxiliary online numerical optimization. Notably, the proposed splitting method guarantees a reduction in variance along a target level-set-point direction of a Gaussian particle. This enables accurate and efficient propagation of particles. We show that AMF consistently outperforms various baseline filters across diverse benchmarks, including single and coupled slow-fast Van der Pol oscillators and the Lorenz attractor. We also propose a parallel implementation of AMF, allowing high-fidelity PDF estimation with practical computational cost.

Abstract: Finite element methods are among the most widely used discretizations for flow in porous media, and their discrete fracture models (DFMs) for highly conductive fractures are well-established. In this paper, we propose a simple extension of the linear finite element method for modeling low-permeability barriers through a closed-form modification of the local stiffness matrix of each barrier-cut element. The method retains exactly the same $H^1$-conforming $P^1$-finite element space, preserves the original sparsity pattern and symmetric positive definiteness, requires no mesh fitting, and coincides with the standard finite element method away from barriers. Moreover, an inexpensive, purely local post-processing step recovers the discontinuous pressure field with a sharp jump at the barrier interface, thereby removing the one-element-wide smearing present in the continuous solution. Numerical experiments affirm the theoretical findings and illustrate the practical performance of the related adaptive algorithm driven by the proposed functional error estimator.

Method: 提出一个显式且可解释的正则化器，参数化为表面积与平均曲率相关的几何先验。为最小化该变分模型，设计了针对伽马噪声保真度项定制的镜像下降（mirror descent）算法。理论方面，利用定义在o-极小结构（o-minimal structures）中函数的Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质，证明迭代序列全局收敛到临界点。

Abstract: We propose a deep learning framework for image restoration from images degraded by both multiplicative Gamma noise and blur. Unlike conventional deep equilibrium (DEQ) models that rely on implicit neural regularization, the proposed method learns an explicit and interpretable regularizer parameterized by geometric priors associated with surface area and mean curvature. To minimize the resulting variational model, we develop a mirror descent algorithm tailored to the commonly used Gamma-noise fidelity terms. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz property for functions defined in $o$-minimal structures, we establish the global convergence of the generated iterates to a critical point. Experimental results on both grayscale and color image restoration demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative model-based approaches while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art DEQ models based on implicit regularization, despite requiring substantially fewer trainable parameters.

Abstract: Chirality plays an important role in many optical phenomena, and it is crucial to have efficient and accurate numerical algorithms to simulate solutions in this setting. In this paper, we discuss a High-Order Spectral method coupled to geometric regular perturbation theory, which results in just such a method. We view the chirality of the laterally periodic, constant permittivity/permeability structure as deviating from a background value, and demonstrate analyticity of the field scattered by a plane electromagnetic wave with respect to this deformation. This analyticity is not only with respect to chirality deformations of arbitrarily large real size, but also joint in the spatial variables. We also show how the High--Order Perturbation of Envelopes (HOPE) recursions, which we used to establish these results, can be implemented as a rapid, robust, and reliable numerical algorithm.

### [57] [Data generated internal solutions for the plasma wave equation: error bounds and numerical experiments in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04989)
*Vladimir Druskin,Shari Moskow,Mikhail Zaslavsky*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维时域等离子体波动方程内部解的数值重构问题。该方法基于数据驱动的Gramian矩阵（又称质量矩阵）的Cholesky分解，利用已知背景快照变换得到内部解。在二维MIMO设置下，需要块Gramian矩阵，且边界源数量随着时间采样细化而增加。作者证明了通用的误差界适用于该设置，并通过数值实验验证了相对误差以√τ速率收敛、绝对误差在L¹中趋于零，且该方法对高对比度复合介质依然保持精度。针对噪声数据导致质量矩阵非正定的问题，比较了对角平移与特征值下限两种正则化策略，发现特征值下限方法更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一维情形下，数据驱动的内部解已在L²中证明以√τ阶收敛。本文旨在将该方法推广到二维场景，这是实际应用更为关键的设置。二维情形需要MIMO框架和块Gramian矩阵，边界源数量随采样细化增加，带来了新的理论和技术挑战。此外，噪声数据导致质量矩阵可能失去正定性，需要有效的正则化策略，这是实际应用中不可回避的问题。

Method: 使用Cholesky分解数据驱动的Gramian（质量）矩阵，将已知背景快照变换为内部解。在二维场景中采用多输入多输出（MIMO）架构，使用块Gramian矩阵，且边界源数量需随时间采样细化而增加。理论分析将一般误差界推广至该设置，证明数据生成解与背景快照最优因果逼近之间的距离由最优逼近的质量矩阵失配控制。针对噪声导致质量矩阵非正定的问题，比较了对角平移和特征值下限两种正则化策略。

Result: 在正方形域上的数值细化研究显示，数据生成解与背景快照最优因果逼近的收敛性得到验证：相对误差以√τ速率趋于零，绝对误差在L¹中趋于零。对于高对比度复合介质，解重构仍保持精度。在噪声影响方面，当噪声幅度达到数据均方根振幅的10%时，特征值下限正则化方法比对角平移更稳健，重构解仍比未扰动的背景场更精确。

Conclusion: 本文成功将基于Cholesky分解的数据驱动内部解重构方法从一维推广到二维MIMO设置，验证了误差界的普适性。该方法的收敛特性（相对误差√τ阶、绝对误差L¹收敛）在数值上得到确认，且对高对比度介质仍然有效。在噪声环境下，特征值下限正则化策略优于对角平移，提供了更稳健的重构结果。

Abstract: We consider the computation of internal solutions for a time domain plasma wave equation with an unknown potential $q$ from boundary response data. The internal solutions are computed by transforming known background snapshots using the Cholesky decomposition of the data-driven Gramian, or mass matrix. It was recently shown that in one dimension these data generated internal solutions converge in $L^2$ at order $\sqrtτ$ for well chosen initial waves. Here we study the internal solution reconstruction in two dimensions, where a multiple input/multiple output (MIMO) setup and a block Gramian are needed, with the number of boundary sources increasing as the time sampling is refined. We show that the general error bound carries over to this setting: the distance between the data generated solutions and the best causal approximation from background snapshots is controlled by the best approximation mass matrix mismatch. Numerical refinement studies on a square domain measure the convergence of the data generated solutions alongside the best causal approximation from the background snapshots, with the relative errors appearing to go to zero at rate $\sqrtτ$, and the absolute errors going to zero in $L^1$. We also show that the solution reconstructions remain accurate for high contrast composite media. Finally, since the mass matrix assembled from noisy response data can fail to be positive definite, regularization is needed; comparing a diagonal shift with an eigenvalue floor, we find the floor more robust, with reconstructions that remain more accurate than the unperturbed background field with noise that is up to ten percent of the root mean square data amplitude.

</details>

### [58] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C²MOE框架，通过信息论视角将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性组件，用于处理对话情感识别中模态缺失问题。该框架采用交互感知专家、双分支预测机制和可学习重加权模块，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话情感识别（MERC）依赖完整的多模态输入，但真实场景中常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重降低模型性能。现有方法通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: C²MOE将表示学习和缺失模态插补统一在一个信息论框架中：(1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性组件——一致性通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕捉，互补性通过最大化模态间条件熵保留；(2) 引入双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独有线索；(3) 采用可学习重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现鲁棒自适应的融合插补。

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C²MOE在各种缺失模态设置下均持续超越现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 同时考虑模态一致性和互补性的信息论分解框架能有效解决多模态情感识别中的模态缺失问题，实现更鲁棒和自适应的缺失模态插补。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [59] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限温度连续自旋感知机在Gaussian混合数据上训练的变分方法，通过插值方法结合log-concavity和集中估计，推导出淬灭压力的上下minimax变分界，两个界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，当优化可交换时两者匹配并给出模型的完整解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图在更广泛的模型设定下（连续自旋感知机、Gaussian混合数据、广义凹效用函数和log-concave可分离先验度量）统一求解该统计物理模型的淬灭自由能，并同时覆盖基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差的计算，弥补现有方法在该类问题上的缺口。

Method: 将插值方法与log-concavity及集中不等式（concentration estimates）相结合，推导淬灭压力的下界和上界变分表达式；利用变势能的凹-凸结构控制所有剩余极值，两个界仅在对两个变分参数的最优化顺序上存在差异。当两个优化可交换时上下界吻合，同一变势能还提供不动点方程作为驻点条件。

Result: 推导出有限温度连续自旋感知机在Gaussian混合数据上的上下minimax变分界，两个界仅差变分参数优化顺序；当优化可交换时上下界匹配并确定模型解；统一方法可用于计算基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为连续自旋感知机模型提供了统一的边界分析工具，揭示了变分参数优化顺序与解唯一性之间的深刻联系，并为后续精确解的寻找指明了条件（即优化可交换性）。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文通过分析稀疏编辑在最小生成树（MST）上的传播机制，证明成对超度量值仅在两种情况下发生变化：树路径穿越被编辑的边，或路径穿越因编辑离树边而新暴露的割。据此定义了逐编辑暴露割分数和树全局包络，推导出Hamming-Lipschitz界，并通过构造严格割分离和离树编辑的显式示例验证界的锐度。

Conclusion: 文章表明稀疏编辑对超度量（单链层次聚类表示）的影响规模由树几何结构决定，而非单纯由编辑规模决定，这一依赖是本质不可回避的。所提出的结构分数对层次表示的安全性和鲁棒性诊断具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计优化器在实践中表现优异但缺乏统一的机理理解，其行为在不同场景下难以解释和预测。作者希望通过一个信任域框架，将自适应优化中的多种看似不同的技术（矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量低通滤波、谱归一化）统一起来，以提供一个理论性的理解视角。

Method: 作者构建了一个信任域优化框架，其中每个权重的更新步长被限制在一个由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）所定义的信任域内。从这个框架出发，推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制，称为Gmake。当p=4时该机制涉及峰度（kurtosis）估计。该框架将归一化、学习率调度、动量形式的谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化统一在同一个信任域视角下。

Result: 在GPT2-124M模型上（在FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集上训练）的实验表明，四阶矩实现在信任域约束较弱时提供最大收益；随着逐渐引入更强的信任域控制，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到比对应四阶矩实现略低的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任域框架为理解自适应矩估计机制提供了一个统一的理论视角。四阶矩（峰度）估计在弱约束场景下有益，但在强约束下二阶矩实现可能更优甚至表现更好，这表明不同阶矩的选择应与信任域约束的强度相匹配。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>

Result: 模型在训练/验证集上达到约93%的分类准确率，但进一步分析表明该准确率指标夸大了模型的泛化能力。即使加入了额外训练数据，模型仍无法可靠地泛化到此前未见过的新同位素。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [63] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一理论框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学联系起来，解释深度网络为何能泛化。核心发现是"熵可学习性视界"(ELH)——一个决定网络能否真正学习目标函数的基本定律，并据此提出熵梯度下降(EGD)算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络的泛化能力与经典理论(VC维、Rademacher复杂度)预测严重矛盾，理论预测灾难性过拟合而现实中模型却泛化良好。作者旨在弥合这一理论鸿沟，建立更准确的深度学习极限理论。

Method: 引入熵可学习性视界(ELH)统一框架：只有当数据流形的Shannon熵超过决策边界的拓扑熵（由网络权重空间的von Neumann熵所平衡）时，网络才能学习目标函数。证明Shannon-拓扑瓶颈定理，描述熵相变和"信息挫折"(Informational Frustration)玻璃态记忆相的出现，并将grokking现象重新解释为"熵释放"。

Result: 建立了Shannon-拓扑瓶颈定理，证明当决策边界几何复杂度超过信息视界时系统发生突变性熵相变，进入信息挫折的玻璃态记忆相，此时泛化热力学上不可能。将grokking识别为熵释放过程，并提出了熵梯度下降(EGD)算法来动态管理权重熵以维持学习进程。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵从不确定性追踪工具重新定位为决定机器能否学习的基本物理货币，提供了一种理解深度学习泛化极限的新范式。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>

*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法（如变分法和集合卡尔曼滤波）依赖于贝叶斯推断来更新数值预报模型状态，但通常针对不同同化任务（滤波vs平滑）需要不同的算法框架，缺乏统一性。研究者希望提出一种从根本上不同的、统一的方法来应对多种数据同化任务。

Method: 使用潜在视频流匹配模型，在ERA5再分析数据（覆盖8天窗口、69个变量）上训练先验；通过后验采样（posterior sampling）吸收来自NOAA综合无线电探空仪档案和综合地表数据库的真实观测数据。由于先验生成连续轨迹，信息自然在观测帧和未观测帧之间传播，仅通过改变观测帧即可实现滤波和平滑等不同同化任务。

Result: 该方法能够直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，其性能与最先进的观测到预报（observation-to-forecast）模型相当，表明该方法在预报性能上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于潜在视频流匹配的统一数据同化框架可以同时处理滤波、平滑和直接从观测生成集合预报等多种任务，为大气数据同化提供了一种新范式，且在预报性能上达到与专门设计的观测到预报模型相竞争的水平。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [65] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lindblad方程启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（LMR），通过将递归算子拆分为精确离散化的阻尼旋转模式，使旋转（相位混合）与衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立设计变量，并提供直接的全局稳定性保证，在多种基准任务上达到最优或竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络将信号混合、记忆保留和稳定性耦合在单个随机递归矩阵中，缺乏对可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的分开模态控制，也无法直接提供全局稳定性保证。尽管已有结构化设计（如正交、泄漏、深度等）改进了拓扑、范数保持等，但都未同时实现独立的旋转/衰减控制与显式稳定性保证。

Method: 基于经典Lindblad方程原理，构建多时间尺度储备池。递归算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模式组装而成，旋转与衰减分别控制相位混合和记忆损失。通过正交模式混合保持算子正规性，衰减谱直接决定echo-state稳定性裕度，无需事后调整谱半径。与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环、下一代储备池及紧凑训练GRU进行对比评估。

Result: 在10个对齐种子的基准套件上，所提储备池在受限NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，与最强线性记忆结果持平，并在其他基准上保持广泛竞争力。消融实验表明旋转增强状态多样性，耗散提供可控遗忘并改善预测条件数。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可解释的递归架构，其中混合、记忆和稳定性是显式且独立可调的设计变量，弥合了开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [66] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将权重分布约束从"缩小解空间的限制"重构为"定义学习内在几何的度量结构"，将深度神经网络和变分量子电路建模为Wasserstein流形上的梯度流，并提出了Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo两种算法，通过实验证明尊重这些分布几何能够改善泛化、稳定训练并缓解量子变分电路中的贫瘠高原问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的统计力学学习理论将权重分布约束视为缩小解空间、降低模型容量的限制。作者希望采取相反的视角，借鉴分布约束感知机（distribution-constrained perceptrons）的早期工作，将预设的权重分布视为定义学习自然展开的内在几何，而非单纯的限制。

Method: 将深层神经网络和变分量子电路表述为Wasserstein流形上的梯度流——每一层对应一个经典Wasserstein空间，电路参数对应一个量子Wasserstein空间。开发了深层网络的层次化平均场描述，利用一阶量子Wasserstein距离扩展到量子领域，并提出了两种实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo，它们沿着乘积流形上的近似测地线进行优化。

Result: 在teacher-student问题、标准图像分类任务和小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明，与无约束和纯范数基线相比，尊重这些分布几何能改善泛化性能、稳定训练，并减少贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该方法首次将结构约束重新定义为几何先验，为将生物、光谱或硬件推导的分布信息纳入经典和量子两类学习系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [67] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Open-World Learning (OWL)油井异常检测流水线下游部署一个大语言模型代理层，利用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（通过NVIDIA NIM服务）作为上游方法的"伴侣"而非替代品，为检测结果提供自然语言解释、置信度排序的批判性反馈以及新异簇的合并命名，以弥合可解释性差距，促进OWL流水线在实际运营场景中的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OWL流水线（自动编码器检测+多分类+基于马氏距离的新颖性检测）虽然能回答"发生了什么"，但不能解释"模型为什么这样认为"或"操作员下一步应该做什么"，且无法为新异簇提供人类可读的命名。这种可解释性差距阻碍了OWL流水线在运营环境中的实际部署。

Method: 在OWL流水线下游添加一个大语言模型代理层，使用通过NVIDIA NIM服务的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B混合专家模型。代理接收结构化传感器指标及上游分类/新颖性断言，返回自然语言理由、置信度排序的评判意见，以及新异类别的合并命名。代理的角色包括：确认合理的上游决策、提供可审计的传感器依据解释、标记不合理标签的分歧、为新异事件命名。

Result: 在来自3W数据集的989个真实井段上进行了三项研究：全部9个类别上top-1准确率35.1%/top-3准确率63.9%（95%CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类别top-2验证准确率71.7% [64.8, 77.6]，精确度0.91 [0.84, 0.95]；新颖性检测89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，并在7个隐藏类别中的5个上实现了稳定的簇命名。

Conclusion: LLM代理并非独立的分类器，而是作为OWL流水线的伴侣层，通过确认、解释、标记分歧和命名四个核心功能，弥合了OWL方法在可解释性方面的缺口，从而推动其从研究走向实际运营部署。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [68] [Sample Complexity of Multicalibration for Multilevel Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04288)
*Jiuyao Lu,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Aleksandr Podkopaev,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多校准（multicalibration）在序列属性（sequential properties）框架下的样本复杂度。对于k个属性的序列，其中每个属性在前序属性固定后可辨识，作者证明了在规则条件下，匹配的上下界：多校准误差ε需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本，同时给出了使用O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)样本的随机学习器，因此对于多项式大小的组族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多预测任务需要预测同一条件结果分布的多个相关特征，如方差（相对均值定义）、偏度（相对均值和方差定义）以及条件风险价值（相对分位数定义）。多校准通常要求预测器在多个组上同时保持无偏性，但现有工作主要关注单一属性（如均值或分位数）。作者希望建立统一的框架来研究多个序列相关属性的多校准，并理解其样本复杂度。

Method: 作者定义了一个包含k个属性的序列框架，其中每个属性在给定前序属性后是可辨识的（identifiable），该框架包含贝叶斯对（Bayes pairs）但不要求属性来自单一损失函数。理论上，作者为每个固定的k≥2建立了匹配的上下样本复杂度界（至多差对数因子），包括构造一个反例证明即使是多项式对数个二元组也需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本，以及给出一个对任意有限组族G的随机学习器。

Result: 证明多校准误差ε在多项式对数个二元组下需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本（下界）；给出了使用O(ε^{-(k+2)}+ε^{-2}log|G|)个样本的随机学习器（上界）。对于多项式大小的组族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})。该理论在三个典型示例（如均值-方差、矩序列、分位数-条件风险价值等）上进行了实例化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了序列属性多校准问题的完整样本复杂度刻画，显示k个属性的多校准样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})，其中k=1时退化为已知的经典多校准结果。该框架统一了多种预测特征并通过三个典型示例展示了其适用性。

Abstract: Calibration requires a predictor to be unbiased after conditioning on its own predictions. Multicalibration asks for this guarantee simultaneously across a collection of groups. Many prediction tasks ask for several related features of the same conditional outcome distribution: variance is defined relative to the mean, skewness relative to both mean and variance, and conditional value at risk relative to a quantile. We study multicalibration for a sequence of $k$ properties in which each property is identifiable once the preceding properties are fixed. This framework includes Bayes pairs but does not require the properties to arise from a single loss.
  For every fixed $k\ge2$, we establish matching upper and lower sample-complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors under regularity conditions. Even with only polylogarithmically many binary groups, achieving multicalibration error $\varepsilon$ requires $\widetildeΩ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ samples. Conversely, for any finite group family $\mathcal G$, we give a randomized learner using $O(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\log|\mathcal G|)$ samples. Thus the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ for polynomial-size group families. We instantiate the theory for three canonical examples.

</details>


### [69] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个开源的触觉压力阵列感知模型，仅凭压力数据即可回答文本查询。在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062 top-1准确率（top-3为0.935），超越有监督闭环CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。模型采用小数据配方：187条训练记录、144k无标签帧上掩码自编码器预训练、以及传感器校准仿射变换——后者贡献的精度提升超过所有架构改动总和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是成本最低、出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表示学习研究几乎全部聚焦于光学触觉传感器（通过成像形变凝胶）。这种研究偏好导致压力阵列这一主流传感器缺乏相应的表示学习方法，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 采用小数据训练配方：(1) 仅187条训练记录；(2) 在144k张无标签同传感器帧上进行掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练；(3) 利用传感器自身的校准仿射变换进行数据处理。没有训练分类头，直接通过文本查询进行零样本/少样本匹配。模型接入一个开放的记忆层。

Result: 在STAG基准（27个对象、留出录制集）上，四次运行平均top-1准确率为0.771±0.062，top-3为0.935，匹配甚至超过有监督闭环CNN的0.76。误差集中在少数接触模糊类别，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05, 702对类别），对改写甚至裸名称查询鲁棒。两帧多样帧即可恢复八帧准确率的89%。同时诚实报告失败案例：跨传感器预训练池化无增益、视觉协同训练反而损伤触觉表现、错误归一化输入管线静默丢弃97%动态范围。

Conclusion: Tactus证明了压力阵列传感器在触觉表示学习中的价值被严重低估。通过小数据配方和传感器固有校准信息，可以在无分类头的情况下达到甚至超越有监督方法的性能。同时，论文以同等精度报告失败与成功，为触觉表示学习提供了可复现的基准和开放资源。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined.
